# RANKED-VISITOR — the visitor's pool, a one-off measure beside hxd (SAME-2 brief D part 3)

Tree **7e93c087b (src/; the visitor's plan rendered by the renderer's --plan birth, committed with this file)**. Reading: `reading.visitor.json` from the run `door/visitor` (150 places a size, six sizes), measured with `scripts/sameness-census.mjs :: measureSlots` (the door's slot key and attribution); no new render. Classes are the register's (`?` = the rule's guess).

## A SENTENCE REPEATED INSIDE ONE PLACE (the chair, 20:14)

Measured by `scripts/sameness-census.mjs :: repeatsInPlace` over every place of the run: a sentence printed twice or more inside ONE place within one surface FAMILY (the web dossier is one family, each PDF form one, the session sheet one), at any positions, the same position included. Sentences are MASKED (each place's names to `#`, digits to `0`), so one wording printed twice with other names in it counts: the frame repeats. SIDE: STORY where a position's register class is STATE or NARRATIVE (the fault), FURNITURE where every position is CATALOGUE or LABEL (a house's name or a caption twice is not the fault), UNROWED where no position has a register row (fewer than 5 places print it). Every repeated sentence, per size, is in `REPEATS.tsv` beside this file.
